# Deal Expring Soon=곧 만료되는 거래 #건
Begins in {{A}} hrs={{A}}시간 후 시작
Begins in {{A}} min={{A}}분 후 시작
Expires in {{A}} min={{A}}분 후 만료
Expires in {{A}} hrs={{A}}시간 후 만료
You've got a decent base of operations and a growing customer base. You'll be pulling in some proper cash now, but you'll need help to manage all your customers.=괜찮은 거점도 생겼고 고객층도 늘고 있네.\n이제 제법 돈이 들어오겠지만, 고객들을 전부 관리하려면 도움이 필요할 거야.
Time to think about recruiting a <color\=#4CB0FF>dealer</color>. There's a guy... hmm... <color\=#4CB0FF>Benji Coleman</color> his name is, I think. He'll be the first dealer you can work with.=<color\=#4CB0FF>딜러</color>를 영입할 때가 됐어.\n어떤 녀석이 있는데... 음... 이름이 <color\=#4CB0FF>벤지 콜먼</color>이었나.\n네가 처음으로 같이 일할 수 있는 딜러야.
You won't be able to hire a dealer until one of their friends has recommended you. Benji is friends with <color\=#4CB0FF>Peggy, Beth,</color> and <color\=#4CB0FF>Ludwig</color>. Focus on selling to them until one of them recommends you.=딜러 친구 중 한 명이 널 추천하기 전까진 그 딜러를 고용할 수 없어.\n벤지는 <color\=#4CB0FF>페기, 베스,</color> 그리고 <color\=#4CB0FF>루트비히</color>와 친구야.\n그중 하나가 널 추천할 때까지 걔들한테 파는 데 집중해.
You've finished a few deals and made some decent cash, but if you're looking to make real money, you'll need to expand your operations - more <color\=#4CB0FF>product</color> and more <color\=#4CB0FF>customers</color>.=거래도 몇 건 끝냈고 돈도 좀 만졌겠지만, 진짜 큰돈을 벌고 싶으면 사업을 확장해야 해 - 더 많은 <color\=#4CB0FF>제품</color>과 더 많은 <color\=#4CB0FF>고객</color> 말이야.
The best way to gain new customers is to give a <color\=#4CB0FF>free sample</color> to a friend of an existing customer.=새 고객을 얻는 가장 좋은 방법은 기존 고객의 친구에게 <color\=#4CB0FF>무료 샘플</color>을 주는 거야.
